## Volume 04, Batch 0001 — Chapters 145–154

**Window: Friday 15 May 2015 to Friday 5 June 2015. Ten chapters, three weeks. THE SERIES IS AT 154 OF 720. VOLUME 04 IS *EMERGENCY POWERS*, CHAPTERS 145–192, AND IT IS NOT CLOSED AND IT RUNS ON FOR THIRTY-EIGHT MORE CHAPTERS. `outline/volume-03.md` AND `outline/volume-04.md` BOTH DO NOT EXIST AND ARE STILL OWED, AND NOTHING IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS MAY BE BACK-FITTED INTO EITHER.**

**WHAT THIS BATCH DID, IN ONE PARAGRAPH. It started a different kind of book in the same city without pretending that Volume 03 did not happen. It put a heat wave, a chemical spill and the first seed of a written request for one central thing into an ordinary fortnight of weather, water, linen and a school, and it brought back three people who had not appeared since Volume 02 and gave each of them a reason of their own and a refusal that was theirs. It staged the hour between six and seven once and only once. It found a file that was carried and not dealt with, in a tray, under post, on a second floor, and did not open it. It had a person who was not required to be told anything put one correct sentence about a catch tray into a drawer with no key on it, and nobody in the borough knows it is there, including him. It had one person granted one narrow written thing by a person who could say no, in about eleven seconds, and he saw the shape of it and could not stop it. It refused to help anybody. THE REGISTER DID NOT MOVE ONCE. A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED, AND IT IS HEAVIER, AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE REFUTED IN THIS VOLUME EITHER.**

**CHAPTER 0145, FRIDAY 15 MAY — A HOT FORTNIGHT.** The heat arrives as an ordinary Friday of it and the first thing in this borough that fails is not dramatic: it is water, and then it is a laundry, and then an extract in a wing nobody has had a budget for. The finding: **every instrument in this place is an instrument for finding out who is owed something, and a heat wave does not distinguish, and everybody in a hot week is owed the same thing and almost nobody in this borough is owed anything at all, and so every instrument in it is pointed the wrong way, and it kept working perfectly, and it produced nothing.** The counter refused twice, both correctly, about a fan and about a landlord, and neither refusal is a service. **On the second floor at half past one, a woman carrying a tray out of the sun found a buff file at the bottom of it — REPORT OF A RELEASE, MARKET YARD, BAY 2, 14 OCTOBER 2011, CLOSED — and put the tray back exactly as it was and told nobody.** He was on the ground floor and knows none of it and is not going to be told.

**CHAPTER 0146, SATURDAY 16 MAY — BAY TWO.** A market in a heat, a contractor's van on a loading bay, a coupling that came apart, about sixty litres across a concrete apron into a gully that goes under a food market, and a wheel stop in front of the grate that is the only thing in that yard anybody can see the purpose of. **The first person on the scene was a man with a bucket and a reason — the man who turns a bucket at the board of forty slots — and what he did was correct and it was not recognised and it will not be, and he was not thanked.** A bucket and a boot and about two and a half minutes, and then he put the bucket back on the barrow and went to wash it. **AND JONAS MERCER WAS FOUR HUNDRED YARDS AWAY ON PURPOSE FOR ABOUT A QUARTER OF AN HOUR AND DID NOT GO, AND BOTH OF THOSE THINGS ARE GOING TO STAY; the reason he has said out loud is that the finder is the handle, and the reason he has not said out loud is that he would have been very good at it.** Nobody died. Nobody was rescued. Nothing was lucky. Nine traders threw stock away and a woman with asthma went home in an ambulance for something that was not serious, and she had been in that yard for half an hour when it started, and nobody is going to be told about that in any document at all.

**CHAPTER 0147, SUNDAY 17 MAY — A TERM IS WORTH A SUPPLY.** The hour between six and seven, staged once in this batch and once is all it is getting, with the reversal on the page once — he said in May it was the last time and he is in it again and he is not going to say why — and with his own counting of the wet pavement closed at eight because a count is a rota with one name in it. **THE FINDING, AND IT IS NOT A DISCOVERY: A TERM IS NOT WORTH A DOOR. A TERM IS WORTH A SUPPLY.** The gate is up for a health van, a doctor, a cold box, a day list and a bag of samples, and not one of the eleven people in that waiting area is the reason the van is on the apron, and a supply is worth nothing at all to a person who cannot name it. The sixteenth term is not named, not dated and is given no room.

**CHAPTER 0148, MONDAY 18 MAY — WHAT THE NUMBER IS.** **SANAA IQBAL, 35, A PHYSICIAN, ENTERS THIS BATCH IN A CLINIC, IN A HEAT, WITH A PROBLEM SHE CAN NAME, AND SHE IS NOT HERE FOR HIM.** Eighty-one beds on a hardboard board, a corridor with no mechanical extract, and a thermostat on twenty-two in a plant room that anybody in this city can walk in and read. **She asked him the first question anybody has asked him since 27 April, in a room and not in a public room, and he answered it, and it is the only question he is answered in the next nine days.** Her finding, given to him in her own words and worth more than anything he has been given in nine months: **a number on a board is what a building is held to, and eighty-one beds is a number of beds, and the number they need is a number of people, and the difference between those two numbers is a human being, and a human being does not go in a box.** And then she refused, before he asked, anything about the man who did the sheet on the west site for about eleven weeks and stopped in October, and the refusal is about a person, and it is correct, and it is not one of the eleven and it is not a twelfth. **Nobody was helped. Paragraph 4.11 is never numbered, never stated, never answered. The hand that puts the strip in the drawer was not found and was not asked and was not looked for.**

**CHAPTER 0149, TUESDAY 19 MAY — THE WORD ON THE COVER.** The file is not opened and nobody was asked to reopen it and nobody may be compelled to be told anything, including a correction. The word on the cover was enough. **On the second floor the woman who comes down to the ground floor at ten and at three could not be put on a rota and could not put herself on one, because a rota is not a piece of paper — a rota is a person who cannot say no.** He went to the yard at two and saw the grate and the wheel stop and the padlock that is not rusted, and the padlock has been changed since November and nobody in this city has ever written down what the padlock is for, and the man who used to answer for it is still a market porter after thirty years and was not asked. **AND AT TEN PAST THREE ON TUESDAY AFTERNOON, ON HER OWN TIME, WITH A BAG, IN A HEAT, SHE CAME OFF A BUS AND WALKED INTO THAT YARD AND WROTE ONE SENTENCE, IN ONE HAND, DATED, WITH NO NAME ON IT INCLUDING HERS, ABOUT A CATCH TRAY AT A LOW CORNER THAT WAS NOT THERE, AND ABOUT A REPORT IN A TRAY THAT HAS NOT BEEN OPENED — AND IT WENT INTO THE DRAWER UNDER THAT COUNTER UNDER THE TRAY AND BEHIND THE POST AT TEN THE NEXT MORNING, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME ANYTHING IN THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN GOT RIGHT ON THE RECORD BY A PERSON WHO WAS NOT REQUIRED TO, AND NOBODY KNOWS IT IS THERE AND HE IS NOT GOING TO BE TOLD, AND THE PRICE OF IT IS NOT GOING TO BE PAID BY HER.**

**CHAPTER 0150, WEDNESDAY 20 MAY — A KINDNESS.** **PIA SANTOS, 19, COURIER, KEEPER OF NINE ROUTE MARKERS THAT ARE ON NO SHEET, ENTERS WITH A LIST OF ACTUAL PEOPLE AND A HEAT AND ENTERS BY REFUSING HIM SOMETHING: she will not be a counter, and she will not give him the list, and the reason is not safety — THE LIST IS NOT FOR A BUILDING, THE LIST IS SO THAT WHEN ONE OF US SAYS A STREET, THE OTHER EIGHT KNOW WHICH STREET.** She has a name for the sentence of 27 April and the name is *a kindness* and she is right and she is not interested in the sentence, and she can give him the reason in about ten words: a kindness is a thing somebody does to somebody from outside, nobody is owed it, you cannot build a floor out of it, and if you stop doing it you have not broken a promise because you never made one. **And then she gave him one thing she did not have to give, and it made one of his findings smaller, and he let it: the number of people in a street is not the number of houses, because two of the vans in that street are not anybody's address, and the difference is not an error, it is the street.** A person who is not counted is not a person who is helped was not refuted and was not touched, and no moment in that chapter put a word in his mouth about Lowglass, and she corrected him and he let her, and **he did not say to her the true sentence he has been saying in rooms, and the chapter says on the page why, and she watched him not say it and did not ask.**

**CHAPTER 0151, THURSDAY 21 MAY — ONE NARROW THING.** A laundry round of twenty-two stops, four of them wanted at the front, asked on the back of a delivery note by a woman of about forty-four who is on no list. **Granted, in about eleven seconds, by a person who could have said no, with the cost worked out out loud in four seconds first.** Winifred Crabbe, one of the seven filled seats of a body that has not met since January and cannot require anybody, and not on a rota of that laundry because there is not one. **THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN GIVEN SOMETHING THEY ASKED FOR BY A PERSON WHO COULD SAY NO. THE COST LANDED ON BED NINETEEN AND ON A WOMAN OF ABOUT NINETY-ONE, AND NEITHER OF THEM IS IN THE ROOM AND NEITHER IS GOING TO BE TOLD.** And the shape: a correct request granted once is a precedent, and a precedent does not need anybody to decide anything, and the danger of it is that the next one is easier and the one after that costs less, and there is no form, and he could see it and could not stop it and nobody in that room was going to stop it, and he did not write it down.

**CHAPTER 0152, FRIDAY 22 MAY — WHAT A PERSON CAN BE HELD TO.** **NELL ARDENT, 39, MAINTAINS THE PUBLIC CHARTER RECORD, ENTERS IN A CORRIDOR WITH A BOX ON SOMETHING, AND IT IS NOT UNKIND OF HER TO WANT ONE, AND THAT IS THE DIFFICULTY.** Nine sheets, four empty PARTY boxes, and a record with a box on it is what a person can be held to, and being held to is not always a harm, and the whole of nine months says otherwise and is also not entirely right, and neither of them can prove it. **SHE ASKED HIM WHAT A PERSON IN THIS BOROUGH IS HELD TO AND HE SAID NOTHING, and the answer is a drawer, and nobody may tell her there is one, and the whole of what a drawer is depends on nobody ever going and looking. SHE DID NOT PRESS HIM. HE COULD HAVE WARNED HER IN FOUR SECONDS AND IS NOT GOING TO AND THE REASON IS THAT IF HE WARNED HER SHE WOULD BE THE WOMAN WHO WAS WARNED. NOBODY OPENED A DRAWER.** The not-saying is the whole of the chapter and she watched him not say it and let him not say it.

**CHAPTER 0153, SATURDAY 23 AND SUNDAY 24 MAY — WHO GOES WITHOUT.** A car park at thirty-six degrees with about fifty people in it and one bowser coming on the Sunday and four streets, and no order and no declaration and nothing chairing anything, and the argument went on for two hours and everybody in it was right and a woman of about seventy who came on a bus ended it in four seconds by pointing out that a standpipe is not dry, it is dear. **AND THE BOROUGH HAS NINE PEOPLE WHO CAN BE ASKED, WHICH IS NOT A RECORD AND IS GOING TO BE GONE IN A FORTNIGHT, AND THE PRICE OF IT IS THAT THE NINE ARE FINDABLE, AND ONE OF THEM SAID SO OUT LOUD AND GOT LAUGHED AT AND THE LAUGHING WAS RIGHT.** Then the Sunday: **the hour between six and seven is not stood in, and the difference is on the page — in 0147 eleven people were kept out of a building by the hour, and this morning nine people are in a corridor that is keeping nobody out of anything and six of them brought clipboards and every one of them chose to be there, and an hour that requires nothing can be full, and that is not a finding.** And at half past six a woman with a barrow asked the man who turns a bucket three ordinary questions and he answered all three, and she wrote his face on the back of her hand in pencil because she had no paper. **A man a price makes findable and a man a shape in a room makes findable are two different men and they are not to be put in one sentence, and the woman of about sixty who has said so four times is right, and what happened at half past six on Sunday was neither of those things.**

**CHAPTER 0154, MONDAY 25 MAY INTO FRIDAY 5 JUNE — THE STANDING OF THE CITY.** The week, in a hot fortnight: eighty-one beds and no extract, a laundry that cannot cool, a bay behind a market with a wheel stop and no catch tray and an unopened report on a shelf in a building it has nothing to do with, a street of ninety-one houses with no water and a standpipe somebody pays for by the bucket, a school shut at one o'clock twice in a fortnight and about forty letters back out of ninety, a car park at thirty-six degrees with fifty people in it and four streets, a corridor on a Sunday morning with nine people and six clipboards. **AND ON THE TWENTY-NINTH OF MAY A PAGE WAS PUT IN A TRAY IN A ROOM IN TIDEGLASS WITH THE TWENTY-NINTH OF MAY ON IT AND AN EMPTY SIGNATURE BLOCK AT THE BOTTOM, and it is the smallest object anybody in this city has produced in nine months and it is the largest, and it is not in force, and nobody in this borough knows what is written on it, and Jonas Mercer did not read it and is not going to.** Then the school hall at Tarn Street on Friday 5 June, twenty-nine degrees, about nine people, no agenda on the wall and none since January. **Iven Sore — a she, and canon is unambiguous — got four thousand litres into an empty school tank in about twenty minutes and it was a third of the problem and she said so out loud unasked, and the head teacher worked out that four thousand litres is three days in an open building and nine in a shut one, and the other three buildings on her page are still empty, and nobody was turned away at Tarn Street and nobody got anything at the other three either, and one person made one decision in a corridor and got a third of a problem.** He said one thing, to the wall, about a catch tray that is not at a gully, and the caretaker opened a window. Then the standing of the city on Friday 5 June, spoken, with one item new, and the people, who are not in that and cannot be in it, and the sentence that got heavier, and then three pieces of paper nobody has to deal with — one with post on top of it since 18 February, one in a drawer with no key on it and no list, and one on a page in a tray dated 29 May with an empty signature block — and **the batch ends on a question and not a resolution, and it is not the question this borough asked itself in that vestibule on the thirteenth of May, and it is not about a person: SO WHICH OF THE THREE IS GOING TO BE OPENED FIRST.**

**THE QUESTION AND THE ANSWER COUNT, AND IT IS RIGHT IN EVERY CHAPTER. JONAS MERCER ASKED NO QUESTION OF ANYBODY ON ANY OF THE TEN DAYS AND HAS NOW ASKED NONE SINCE THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF FEBRUARY 2015. HE WAS ASKED TWO QUESTIONS IN NINE DAYS AND ANSWERED ONE.**

**ONE LATTICE NOTICE PER ORDINARY CHAPTER AT MOST, FOUR ACROSS THESE TEN, AND THERE ARE THREE: Chapter 0145 on the wall beside the lift at the Old Ward, Chapter 0147 at eye height in a corridor on level two at Saint Orra west, Chapter 0151 taped inside the door of the laundry at the back of the hospital site. ALL THREE DEFINE NOTHING, REQUIRE NOTHING OF ANYBODY, AND DEFINE *HOLDER* ON NONE OF THEM. The 1 March line was not reissued, restated or quoted. The two conditions on the Old Ward board were not reconciled. The two letters and one word in pencil on the back of the enamel strip were not read and are not going to be.**

**THE SAINT ORRA WEST CHAIN, CORRECTED, BINDING, AND UNCHANGED IN ITS RULE: A TERM IS SIX DAYS. IT IS READ AT 22:00 ON THE SATURDAY NIGHT BEFORE THE SUNDAY IT STARTS ON, THE GATE OPENS AT 07:00 ON THAT SUNDAY, AND IT EXPIRES AT 06:00 ON THE FOLLOWING SUNDAY. THE SATURDAY NIGHTS ARE WHAT ARE A WEEK APART. NEVER CALL THE TERM SEVEN DAYS AND NEVER CALL IT A WEEK.** 24 Jan = 1 · 31 Jan = 2 · 7 Feb = 3 · 14 Feb = 4 · 21 Feb = 5 · 28 Feb = 6 · 7 Mar = 7 · 14 Mar = 8 · 21 Mar = 9 · 28 Mar = 10 · 4 Apr = 11 · 11 Apr = 12 · 18 Apr = 13 · 25 Apr = 14 · 2 May = 15 · **9 May = 16 · 16 May = 17 · 23 May = 18 · 30 May = 19**. **NINETEEN OF THOSE SATURDAY NIGHTS HAVE NOW BEEN REACHED BY THE END OF CHAPTER 154 AND NOT ONE OF THEM HAS A DATE WRITTEN ON IT ANYWHERE IN THIS CITY.** WHO READS THEM IS EZRA MBEKI, IN AND OUT OF THAT BUILDING SINCE 1992, AND TREVOR NUNN, 58, A NIGHT PORTER, NINETEEN YEARS, NOT ON A ROSTER BECAUSE THE ROSTER DOES NOT EXIST. **NO CHAPTER IN THIS BATCH PUT EITHER MAN IN A ROOM, NO HANDOVER WAS STAGED, AND THE FIFTEENTH REMAINS THE LAST ONE EVER STAGED.** **THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN WAS STAGED ONCE IN CHAPTER 0147 AND ONCE IS ALL IT IS GETTING, AND THE SIXTEENTH IS NOT NAMED, NOT DATED, AND HAS NO ROOM.**

**WHAT DID NOT MOVE. THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AT NINETEEN AND DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS, and the blank line under the nineteenth was blank for 170 days as at 15 May, 172 as at 17 May, 176 as at 21 May, 179 as at 24 May, 180 as at 25 May, and A HUNDRED AND NINETY-ONE DAYS as at Friday 5 June 2015, and it is still the only fixed point in that building, and entry nineteen is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out. THE CARD IN THE SLOT was EIGHTY-FOUR DAYS as at 15 May, eighty-six as at 17 May, ninety-three as at 24 May, and A HUNDRED AND FIVE DAYS as at 5 June 2015, and nobody has taken it out and nobody has put it up. THE LETTER IN THE TRAY went in on Wednesday 18 February 2015 at about half past nine and was EIGHTY-SIX DAYS as at 15 May, eighty-eight as at 17 May, ninety-five as at 24 May, and A HUNDRED AND SEVEN DAYS as at 5 June 2015, and it is still sealed and has not been opened in front of a second person and has not been answered, and what is under the post is not known to anybody and may not be said in a room. THE DRAWER UNDER THAT COUNTER IS A DRAWER WITH NO KEY ON IT AND NO LIST, WITH A TRAY ON TOP OF IT THAT IS NOT A LIST, AND IT IS GROWING, AND NOBODY CAN SAY HOW MANY, AND IT NOW HAS ONE THING IN IT THAT NINETEEN YEARS OF DRAWERS HAVE NOT HAD. THE ROTA HAS STILL NOT BEEN REBUILT AND CANNOT BE; THE ROW FROM ELEVEN O'CLOCK TO HALF PAST ONE IS STILL EMPTY AND STILL DOES NOT SAY *VACANT*; BRAM OSEI'S THURSDAY IS STILL REFUSED IN HIS OWN HANDWRITING WITH A SECOND LINE IN SOMEBODY ELSE'S HAND THAT NOBODY LOOKED AT IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND NOBODY IS GOING TO. THE BOARD STANDS AT SEVEN OF NINE WITH TWO CHAIRS, NO CHAIR OF THAT BODY, AND HAS NOT MET SINCE THE MIDDLE OF JANUARY. THE PARAGRAPH WAS NEVER ANSWERED, WAS NEVER NUMBERED AND WAS NEVER STATED. THE HAND THAT PUTS THE STRIP IN THE DRAWER WAS NOT FOUND, NOT ASKED AND NOT LOOKED FOR. ROYDEN ACHEBE WAS NOT TOLD. PETER NAYLOR WAS NOT ASKED ANYTHING. THE MAN OF FORTY-EIGHT WAS NOT ASKED. THE MAN WHO TURNS THE BUCKET WAS NOT FOUND AND WAS NOT INTRODUCED AND WAS ASKED NOTHING BY ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH. THE WEIR IS ON NO LAYER AND NO WEIR RESIDENT IS DESCRIBED IN ANYTHING THAT LEAVES A STREET, BECAUSE NOTHING WAS WRITTEN IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS AT ALL. NOBODY DIED. NOBODY WAS RESCUED. NOBODY WAS HARMED. NOBODY WAS GIVEN A TITLE, A GRADE, A DEPARTMENT, A CHAIR, A SEAT, A MEMBERSHIP, A FORM, A ROTA ROW, A THANK-YOU OR A WAY OF BEING THANKED. NOBODY REFUSED TO BE THANKED AND NO MINUTE RECORDS THAT A PERSON HAS NOT BEEN THANKED. NO NAME WENT IN A BOX IN ANY DOCUMENT.**

a person-box, and the difference of fourteen is a difference between two public numbers and is not fourteen households · Emeka Rusk's, and helping does not go on a form · Margo Doré's advance refusal **AND FOUR THINGS THAT ARE NOT REFUSALS AND ARE NOT ANYBODY'S DUTY: A CARD IN A SLOT THAT NOBODY EMPTIES, A HUNDRED AND FIVE DAYS. A LETTER IN A TRAY UNDER TWO OTHER PIECES OF POST, A HUNDRED AND SEVEN DAYS. A SENTENCE ABOUT A CATCH TRAY IN A DRAWER WITH NO KEY ON IT, WRITTEN BY A PERSON WHO WAS NOT REQUIRED TO. AND A PAGE IN A TRAY WITH AN EMPTY SIGNATURE BLOCK ON IT.**

**THE FIGURES, RECOMPUTED FROM ANCHORS, AND THE CLOSED ONES HELD. The closed elevens hold, with one declared exception: the "about eleven seconds" in Chapter 0151 is the card's own measure of a correct request being granted, it is the same eleven seconds that room has always taken, and it is used once in the header and once in the body and nowhere else in that chapter; the two "answers in about eleven seconds" in the inventories of 0145 and 0154 are the ground-floor counter's established measure, the same figure as the Volume 03 close, and they measure nothing new. Forty is closed and held: no chapter uses the word for a duration. No 48-minute figure. Nine hundred and forty is a number of times, nine hundred and eighty-six is a number of Wednesdays and is not repeated, and fourteen is a difference between two public numbers, and none of the three is a number of people and they have never been in one sentence. "In about four years" was in drafts seventeen times and is five. THE MOTIFS WERE RE-MEASURED AFTER EVERY PROSE CHANGE AND EVERY CHAPTER IS INSIDE THE CAP.**

**REPAIRS APPLIED AFTER REVIEW, AND BINDING, AND THE PROSE WAS CHANGED RATHER THAN THIS FILE. THAT WAS THE FIRST PASS, AND IT FOUND FORTY-FIVE DEFECTS, ALL FORTY-FIVE REPAIRED IN THE CHAPTERS: the sixteenth term was named, dated and given a room and a mechanism in 0147 and is not; the two women of about fifty were merged in 0149 and are not; Chapter 0151 said Winifred Crabbe was not on a board and she is one of the seven filled seats, and the whole of that chapter's finding was re-anchored; 0151 invented a mechanism by which a number enters a record by being spoken aloud; the eighth seat's vacancy was two days out and given the wrong person's refusal in two chapters; the letter in the tray was ninety days old in 0145 and is eighty-six; 0145 anchored the register's quiet period to the wrong day; 0149 misdated the fans question twice; 0149 claimed a public refusal had made the counter's person findable and now says the opposite and better thing; 0152 put a new repair at Salter's Yard; 0150 dated Pia's last sight of him to a year the account does not reach and put him in a doorway he was never in; 0150 said a pad entry named no person two lines after naming two; 0145 restated the man of forty-five's exchange at a new number; 0153 staged a woman of thirty-four; 0153 crowned findability as the fortnight's only success and merged the two kinds that must not be merged; 0154's header said there was nobody left who could ask him anything, which the Volume 03 close corrected out of Chapter 0144; 0153 said fifty-one degrees where 0151 had thirty-one; the tanker at Tarn Street was a rescue by convenience and is now a third of a problem with a cost and three buildings still empty; 0154 named and dated the nineteenth in the clause that said it was neither; the tray and the drawer were merged in two chapters; 0154 put a production rule in the inventory; the nobody-refused-to-be-thanked beat was restated in four headers; the narration referred to itself as a chapter, a batch, a volume and an account in twenty-nine places and all twenty-nine are out; a production constraint was stated inside the fiction in 0150's header; 0145's closing paragraph duplicated the standing of the city; 0146's closing paragraph re-narrated the Volume 03 close near-verbatim; a sentence was used verbatim in three chapters; 0147's counting list named twelve people and said eleven; 0147 attributed four findings to April; two chapters ended on the inventory instead of on a change; 0148 opened on an unparseable answer; 0148 gave one measured act two figures; 0153's "the ninth of them" collided with the ninth seat; 0154 described the ninth seat as a shopkeeper; 0154 had an ungrammatical clause about a fan; 0146 left the batch's one good man's arrival unmotivated; 0154's inventory repeated a removed figure; the substitution "the woman of about fifty" was used for both women in two chapters; and 0149 had a mechanism by which a file and a yard were the same tray.** **NOTHING BELOW TOUCHED THE PLOT, THE THREE RETURNING CHARACTERS, THE REFUSALS, THE GRANT, THE SPILL, THE PAGE, OR THE ENDING QUESTION, AND THE BATCH STILL ENDS ON A QUESTION.**

## Volume 04, Batch 0002 — Chapters 155–164

**VOLUME 04 IS *EMERGENCY POWERS* AND IT IS NOT CLOSED. IT RUNS TO CHAPTER 192 AND THIS BATCH RAN FROM FRIDAY 5 JUNE, WHERE BATCH 0001 ENDED, TO FRIDAY 26 JUNE 2015. TEN CHAPTERS. THE FIRST WEEK OF JUNE IS NOT DATED TO ANY CHAPTER AND NOTHING IN IT IS MINUTABLE, AND THAT IS CORRECT.**

**THE QUESTION AND THE ANSWER COUNT, AND IT IS RIGHT IN EVERY CHAPTER. JONAS MERCER ASKED NO QUESTION OF ANYBODY ON ANY OF THE TEN DAYS AND HAS NOW ASKED NONE SINCE THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF FEBRUARY 2015. HE WAS ASKED ONE QUESTION IN FOUR WEEKS AND ANSWERED IT: a man of thirty-three, a night worker, in a canteen at the back of the Saint Orra Civic Hospital site, on Thursday the eighteenth of June — *is there anybody in this city who is allowed to put me in that sentence without writing anything* — and he said no, with a reason, and it is the only answer he gave in the batch, and it is worth nothing and it is correct. NOBODY ASKED HIM ANYTHING ELSE. He was in a room on 24 June when a page was read off a tray and he did not read it and he was not asked about it and he said nothing. The man of about fifty-six did not ask him a fourth question and did not appear in any of the ten chapters.**

**WHAT THIS BATCH DID, IN THE ORDER A READER MEETS IT. A man with a dog went round nine kitchen doors over three evenings and asked one correct question and got the same correct answer at every one of them, and the answer was nothing, and the reason it was correct is that there is nothing anybody could do with it. A man with a clipboard said out loud in a car park that he was not putting a name on his sheet and gave the right reason. The woman of about thirty-one with a barrow gave the same answer and refused her nine lines to anybody and said that nine lines in a row is a street with a shape. And the man with the dog said the thing nobody had thought of: nine nothings in a row is not nine nothings, it is a street with nothing written against it, and in about four years that will be the only sentence anybody has about the second week of June. A second correct request was granted in a corridor at a school, in one sentence, costing the borough nothing, and that is the second precedent and he could see both and could not stop either. And the first time in nine months that a rota in this borough wanted to include anybody, a head teacher wrote her own name in a column in one movement and nobody noticed except him and he is not on it and cannot be. And then he went and stood in a church hall for three hours because he cannot stop going to places with instruments, and worked out that the two useful things this borough has produced in nine months were both made by hand by people who were not required to, and did not write it down. And a man of thirty-three who read a minute in a corridor in May came back on his own, asked one question, got one correct answer worth nothing, and went to work, and said the correctest thing anybody said in that canteen, and it has not been improved on. And a printed sheet went round a building: four streets, nine lines, every line the same, no name box anywhere on it, and nobody had done anything wrong, and it is the first time in nine months that a correct thing in this borough has been produced by a machine rather than by a person, and the person who could see what it was, in a room where nobody else could, was the one person who was not responsible for it. And a man of about thirty-five slept on a blanket in a stockroom in Tideglass, and a man who does plastering came about twice a month and carried a fan in and said *that'll be about four degrees* and went away, and the shopkeeper put a jug of water on the crate at seven, and nobody can say whether that was help or a delay and neither can the reader, and Jonas Mercer was in the shop and did not do it and the reason is on the page of Chapter 0160 and is about him. And the weather turned on Sunday the twenty-first and the borough was twenty-two degrees on the Tuesday and a man counting a shelf picked up the 2011 file, read the cover, and put it back in the same tray at the same angle, and Jonas Mercer said nothing, and the chapter does not say whether that was right. And a page in a tray was read by a woman of twenty-nine who was not asked to read it, who worked out who wanted it and who would lose and was right about both, who said out loud that the person who reads it is going to be the person who has to do it, and who telephoned the building the next morning to say she had read it. And a correct request was granted in a sentence and, for the first time since the fourth of September, somebody wrote it down — *agreed* and a date on the back of a page — and the bill landed on the filling round nobody has ever claimed, and on a man of sixty at number thirty-one at the end of Collier Lane whose name was said once, in a room, to one other person, with a reason, and who put his hose on at about six on the Friday and there was nothing in the pipe, and who carried four containers two hundred yards twice on the Saturday, and who could not lift the fourth one on the Monday because he is sixty and his right hand has had a bad grip since about February. And Jonas Mercer could have found him in about an hour and did not go, was told in a corridor at about one o'clock in about six words, and said nothing, and Chapter 0163 says why, and the reason is about him and not about the man of sixty. And then the standing of the city, said shorter than it has ever been said, in a first-floor room in Tideglass with seven people in it and no agenda and nobody keeping minutes, with one item new that is not a document and not a form and not a page and is the smallest thing anybody in this borough has produced in nine months: A CHAIR, put out on a landing at about eight in the evening and brought in at about six, by somebody nobody is going to be told about, for about two weeks, at nobody's request. **AND THE BATCH ENDS ON A QUESTION AND NOT A RESOLUTION, AND IT IS NOT THE QUESTION BATCH 0001 ENDED ON AND IT IS NOT THE QUESTION VOLUME 03 ENDED ON AND IT IS NOT ABOUT A PERSON: THERE IS A PAGE IN A TRAY IN THIS BOROUGH THAT ASKS FOR ONE THING TO BE DONE FOR THE WHOLE CITY INSTEAD OF FOR A STREET, AND THERE IS A CHAIR ON A LANDING IN THIS BOROUGH THAT GOES OUT AT ABOUT EIGHT IN THE EVENING AND COMES IN AT ABOUT SIX, AND THE CHAIR IS THE SMALLER OF THE TWO, AND NEITHER OF THEM CAN BE ASKED ABOUT, AND ONE OF THEM HAS A SPACE AT THE BOTTOM FOR A NAME AND THE OTHER ONE HAS NOTHING. SO WHICH OF THE TWO IS GOING TO BE THE FIRST ONE THAT SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH ASKS A PERSON TO AGREE TO.**

**WHAT THIS BATCH DID, IN THE ORDER A READER MEETS IT.** A room on the second floor of the Old Ward was repainted, four trays were under the window, and the woman of about fifty who has been at that counter for eleven years gave three places a tray could go and chose the cupboard under the stairs, because a list costs a person and a tray costs a Friday, and about sixty pieces of dead internal circulars went out of that room in a white van and the cupboard is emptied on Fridays. On Thursday 2 July the filling round at the end of a lane came at about eleven, and a man of about forty-one walked down that lane at half past ten with a form dated the twenty-third of June, and a man of sixty told him he should go away before anybody had asked him anything, and he was right, and the round is not funded and does not get done and nothing is helped. On Monday 6 July a woman of twenty-nine who had read a page in a tray in June asked a man in a supermarket corridor what happens to a person who has read something they were not asked to read, and he said nothing happens and he was right, and then she worked out that the cheapest thing she could do was stop being findable and did it badly by leaving a shop's telephone number on a card in a shop window with nothing else on it, and it cost a woman of about seventy-six a bin she had not carried in since the middle of June. On Tuesday 7 July a shopkeeper of about twenty-four went into his own stockroom for a crate and spent about four minutes looking for a fan that had not been brought back, which is the first time anybody in this borough has gone looking for anything in nine months, and there are two nail holes in that wall now and nobody is ever going to know what was hanging on them. On Wednesday 8 July a man of about forty-six who had read the printed sheet asked for it to go round again with a column added, four people in that room were each right about something different, a column headed *date seen again* was printed with *no name, no initials* under it, and the space is empty and is going to stay empty, and the June sheet was not withdrawn and is still in nine buildings and nobody in any of them knows there are two. On Friday 10 July a counter in a vestibule answered about a thing four times, refused nothing because nothing was asked, told a man of about fifty-six the shape of itself when he asked about a person, and told a man of about forty-seven who was not asking anything three true things about June with the date on each and no name on any. On Monday 13 July one of the four streets was walked from half past two until half past five with nothing at all wrong with it, a man with a price and a card knocked at all nine doors, a man of about sixty-six came up the street with a moving blanket and did a job for nothing, a man of fifty-two opened his door and shut it again, and the printed sheet in another building says that street needs nothing and is right. On Wednesday 15 July a chair went out on a landing at about eight and a man of about seventy-five put a shopping bag on the floor beside a wet chair so a stranger would not have to move it, and the batch noticed, on that landing, in the rain, that this borough has two things with a space for a name on them and two things with nothing on them, and it went nowhere. On Thursday 16 July a man of about forty-two asked for a yard tap for an hour and a porter of about sixty-three said yes in four seconds with no paper on it, and then carried four buckets up a flight of steps in daylight in front of about nine people because the hose did not reach and nobody had asked him to and he could not say no because nothing had been put to him, and a man of thirty-two stood at the back of that yard with a word he does not have and did not use it. And on Friday 17 July the standing of the city was said in a room over a lock-up in Tideglass, in the order of the weeks, with four people in it, in 2,671 words as it now stands, and one thing in it is new and it is two trestle tables and a cardboard box of water bottles outside a shop since about the third of July, which is the second smallest thing anybody in this borough has produced, and the chair is the smallest.**

# Volume 04, Batch 0004 — Chapters 175–184 — Saturday 18 July to Friday 31 July 2015

**DONE. Ten chapters, at one or two days a chapter, and four days in the fortnight are a day nobody wrote down: Monday the twentieth, Friday the twenty-fifth, Monday the twenty-seventh and Wednesday the twenty-ninth of July 2015. THE SERIES IS AT 184 OF 720 AND VOLUME 04 RUNS ON FOR EIGHT MORE CHAPTERS TO 192. THIS IS NOT A VOLUME CLOSE AND IT CLOSES NOTHING.**

**THE BATCH'S FINDING IS ITS CONSTRAINT AND IT IS THE FOURTH FORTNIGHT OF THE SAME SUMMER: a thing that only works while one person is standing in it is not a service and does not become one, and a thing that has now had a second fortnight in which somebody could have asked about it and nobody did has reached the place where a thing becomes a habit. THE NOT-ASKING IS THE FINDING. THE COST OF IT IS THAT THE THING IS IN ITS SECOND MONTH OF BEING PERMANENT AND HAS NOT BEEN NAMED BY ANYBODY, AND THAT A CRATE NOBODY BROUGHT IS NOW A PLACE, AND THAT NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH KNOWS WHERE THAT CRATE CAME FROM — which is the first time in nine months that anybody here has found out that a thing in this borough has an origin nobody can name, and the origin is not a document and not a person anybody can be asked about.**

**THE ENGINE WAS PICKED UP, NOT RESTARTED. THE HEAT BROKE ON 21 JUNE, IT HAD BEEN ABOUT TWENTY-TWO DEGREES SINCE, AND THE TWENTY-THIRD OF JULY WAS THE FIRST DAY IN ABOUT SIX WEEKS THAT ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAD TO THINK ABOUT WEATHER — AND CHAPTER 0179 PUT TWENTY-NINE DEGREES INTO A ROOM ON PURPOSE AND NOT ONE THING IN THAT ROOM MOVED. THE SPILL IS SIX WEEKS OLD AND ITS REPORT IS STILL IN A TRAY UNDER A BUNDLE OF POST ON A SHELF IN A ROOM PAINTED ON 29 JUNE, AND IT WAS NOT OPENED AND NOT LOOKED AT AND THE NUMBER OF PEOPLE WHO HAVE SEEN THE BAY NUMBER AND THE DATE ON THAT COVER IS STILL FOUR.**

**WHAT HAPPENED, IN ORDER. A crate appeared on a pavement nobody brought and a man of sixty-eight said out loud that nobody knows and that a form with a name box on it is worse than a table. A month-old correct sheet was proposed for reprinting and put round every building in the borough by a competent and correct woman, and a duty clerk said no twice for two different correct reasons, and a woman with the keys said no for the reason the sheet actually works, and nothing was carried, and one sentence went out of that room in somebody's mouth and in about a year it is going to cost somebody and the reader is not told. A correct request was granted in three sentences with no paper on it and no reference — a padlock left unlatched on a compound — and the unit that would have fixed a cistern four miles away was released off a board on a Friday, and the cost landed on a woman of fifty-three who cleans that hall herself on a contract with her name on it, and nobody is going to look, and the reason is not discretion and not kindness: she is written down, and a person who is written down to do a thing is a person somebody can ring about it. A man of fifty-nine carried four bags up four floors because he was going down anyway, and a woman of sixty-seven thanked him for a corridor in June, and the thanking did not attach to the bags and nobody put them together, and a man of thirty-two was on the half-landing with nothing in his hands and is the only person in that block who knows both halves. A man of sixty-nine said one true thing about a street he has lived on for twelve years and four people in a room agreed with him, and the agreeing is the cost, and from that afternoon that street has a person in a building who is right about it, and he was asked nothing for the whole time he was there. A cupboard was emptied on a Friday and about sixty pieces of dead paper went into a sack and the sack went into a skip, and that is the first destruction of this borough's paperwork in nine months, and the reader is not told whether that is a loss or a correction. A bench was not looked at and a woman of seventy-one wiped a slat with a cloth out of her own pocket, and nobody in that block is ever going to know that anything needed doing, and the chapter says that is the eleventh time and does not count it. A young man of twenty-one went round about forty doors with no form saying one sentence out loud, and a woman of forty-nine told him she would do it in August on her own front door, and he did not write *declined* and will never know whether she refused him or negotiated with him, and the count of standing refusals did not move and it is eleven and not twelve. A corner shop got a second fridge it could not have justified in April because a card with a telephone number on it has been in its window for three weeks, and the woman who keeps the shop has been asked about that card about thirty times by strangers and has said the same sentence every time and is not going to stop. And since about the twentieth of July there has been a bottle of water on a top step, which is the third smallest thing anybody in this borough has produced, the chair on a landing being the smallest and the two trestle tables smaller than it.**

**ON THURSDAY THE THIRTEENTH OF AUGUST THE CENTRAL CONTINUITY ORDER WAS READ ALOUD IN A FIRST-FLOOR ROOM OVER A SHOP, IN DAYLIGHT, IN FULL, INCLUDING THE PARTS SHE SHOULD HAVE LEFT OUT. **0193 — WHAT AN INSTRUMENT LOOKS LIKE WHEN NOBODY IS WATCHING IT.** A valid instrument had been in force for thirty-seven days and nobody was looking at it, and the finding is that the document has somebody in it and nobody on the street: not one of the seven clauses says who is in charge of a street. Five people on a parade with a trench in it, a man with a stopcock key waiting to be told where to put it, and the man of about thirty-eight on a telephone. About nine people in this borough have stood in front of a copy of the leaked drawing this week and not one of them came to look at the hole in the road, and the hole is the only dangerous thing on that street. **THE SECOND FINDING IS THE CONSEQUENCE OF THE BOUND AND IT IS NOT A RESOLUTION: the field is closed, a closed field cannot take anybody who is not already in it, the clause that would have put anybody new into it has not been applied since about ten to twelve on Friday, and there are five people on a parade of shops where there were thirty the day before, and the work is half done. **THE SECOND CALL ITSELF WENT OFF, AT ABOUT A QUARTER PAST ELEVEN, AND IT WAS NOT CANCELLED, BECAUSE THERE IS NO INSTRUMENT IN THIS CITY THAT CANCELS ANYTHING; WHAT STOPPED THE CALLS IS THAT CLAUSE SIX IS NOT BEING APPLIED, AND THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT PUT NINE PEOPLE TOGETHER IN A DOORWAY AT SEVEN IN THE MORNING ON THE TWENTY-SECOND OF AUGUST, GOT THIRTY ONTO THIS PARADE ON FRIDAY, AND INSIDE THE HALF HOUR AFTER THAT SENTENCE RANG FOUR OF THEM BACK AND SAID ONE. TWO FACTS ARE ON THE SAME PAVEMENT AND THEY HAVE NOTHING TO DO WITH EACH OTHER ON THE PAGE. THERE IS NO PAGE.** **AND THE HARM: a man of about twenty-three, one of the nine, put something in his back on Friday 18 September, went home at the hour he went home, and went past the trench on Saturday carrying a carrier bag at the pace of a man carrying nothing, and Jonas Mercer was about thirty feet away and knew in about a second and a half and did not ask and did not cross the road. EVERYTHING THAT HAPPENED TO HIM IS IN THE FIELD AND EVERY PART OF IT IS CORRECT, AND IT IS A COMPLETE RECORD OF A MAN WHO WAS THERE AND NOT A RECORD OF A MAN WHO IS HURT, AND THE FIELD HAS FOUR THINGS IN IT AND THERE IS NO CLAUSE THAT SAYS THERE COULD BE A FIFTH. NOBODY CAN BE TOLD, BECAUSE THE HURT COULD ONLY ARRIVE AS A DECISION ABOUT WHETHER TO GO ON USING HIM, AND THAT DECISION WOULD BE WRONG AND RIGHT AT ONCE. HE IS ALL RIGHT. HE IS NOT GOING TO BE ASKED.** And he was asked for something for the first time in nine months, and said yes, and held one end of a length of pipe for a minute and a half, and it is not on anything and cannot be recorded. And the bound is holding and is not settled, and nobody won it.

**0194 — WHAT A PLACE LOOKS LIKE THE DAY AFTER ITS OWN STRUCTURE IS PUBLIC.** The road on that parade was made good again at about eleven that morning and nothing that happened on it is going to have to be remembered, and about nine people stood in front of one of the forty sheets and every sentence they said was correct and not one of them is a person the drawing says anything about, and the correct outcome of a shape becoming public is that it is discussed by the people it does not help. **THE SQUARE IS PUBLIC AND EMPTY AND NOBODY CAN SAY WHICH**, and a man with a bicycle put his finger on it and was told he did not know. **He gave two correct answers to a stranger and both of them were useless** — about the sixth clause, about the date on the last page and the line under it and nothing in the line, and about what to do, which is that he could not say because there is nothing in it about what to do. **AND THE LAST THING THAT HAPPENS IS SMALL, CORRECT, MADE OF TWO PEOPLE, AND NOT ON ANYTHING: a man of about twenty-seven with a flatbed and a clipboard gummed a sheet to a shutter, a woman of about sixty-three with two bags held one corner of it for about a minute and did not read it, and neither of them is on anything and nobody is going to be asked about it.** And a thing on a shutter is the first thing in nine months that a person who was not standing there can read about afterwards, and whether that is a proof or a story is still open.

**AND THE ORDER IS IN FORCE AND UNCHANGED AND UNSOFTENED, AND ITS SEVEN CLAUSES AND PAGE TWO WERE NOT ALTERED IN ONE WORD, AND WEDNESDAY 11 NOVEMBER 2015 IS KNOWN, NAMED ON PAGE TWO, AND UNANSWERED, AND THE VOLUME ENDS WITH IT THAT WAY.** Two lattice notices, one in each chapter, against a cap of two, defining nothing and defining *holder* on neither. He was given nothing, nobody offered him anything, nobody refused to be thanked, and the word *warden* was not used of him.

## Volume 05, Batch 0001 — Chapters 195–204 — Thursday 24 September to Tuesday 10 November 2015

**THIS IS THE FIRST BATCH OF VOLUME 05 AND NOT A PHASE OF VOLUME 04, AND THE SERIES IS AT 204 OF 720. IT DELIVERED THE OPENING MOVEMENT ONLY — THE RIVER, THE FIRST PERMISSION, THE FIRST SIGNATURE, THE FIRST THING THAT LEAVES THIS CITY, AND WHAT IT COSTS A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED — AND NOTHING IN IT RESOLVED ANYTHING. NOBODY DIED, NOBODY WAS RESCUED BY ANYBODY ARRIVING, NOBODY WAS RENEWED, NOBODY WAS EXPIRED, NOBODY WAS RENAMED, NOBODY WAS PUT IN A BOX, THE EMPTY SQUARE IS STILL EMPTY, THE LINE UNDER THE ELEVENTH OF NOVEMBER IS STILL EMPTY, WEDNESDAY 11 NOVEMBER 2015 WAS NOT REACHED AND WAS NOT NAMED AND WAS NOT COUNTED DOWN, AND THE ELEVEN STANDING REFUSALS ARE ELEVEN.**

**THE BATCH'S QUESTION, WHICH IS ITS OWN, WHICH IS NOT ABOUT A PERSON, AND WHICH IS NOT ANSWERED: A PERMISSION IS A THING A PERSON MAY DO. AN OBLIGATION IS A THING A PERSON HAS TO DO. THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN LIVING ON THE SECOND ONE FOR NINE MONTHS AND WAS HANDED THE FIRST ONE IN PRINT EIGHTEEN DAYS EARLIER, **WHICH IS WEDNESDAY 30 SEPTEMBER TO SUNDAY 18 OCTOBER AND WAS MEASURED, NOT CARRIED FORWARD; A REVIEW FOUND *SIX DAYS* IN FIVE FILES AND IT WAS NOT AN INTERVAL IN THIS ACCOUNT**, AND NOBODY HAS WORKED OUT WHAT EITHER OF THE MEN ON IT WAS HANDED. IT IS OPEN.** It was said out loud in a room for the first time by Tomas Renn on 18 October, and Jonas Mercer was right about it too, and could not improve it and did not try.

**THE TEN DATES, ALL CHECKED AGAINST THE REAL 2015 CALENDAR, THE GAPS SIX DAYS SIX TIMES AND THEN FOUR, FOUR AND THREE:** 195 Thu 24 Sep · 196 Wed 30 Sep · 197 Tue 6 Oct · 198 Mon 12 Oct · 199 Sun 18 Oct · 200 Sat 24 Oct · 201 Fri 30 Oct · 202 Tue 3 Nov · 203 Sat 7 Nov · 204 Tue 10 Nov. **1,890 / 1,914 / 1,553 / 1,844 / 1,794 / 1,492 / 1,621 / 1,824 / 1,537 / 1,114 WORDS, 16,583 IN THE BATCH, RE-MEASURED WITH `wc -w` AFTER THE REPAIRS RECORDED AT §18 OF THE BATCH 0001 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md`; THE FIGURES THIS LINE REPLACED WERE FIVE SHORT ON EVERY CHAPTER BY A METHOD NOBODY WROTE DOWN, A MEDIAN OF ABOUT 1,675 AGAINST VOLUME 04 BATCH 0005'S 2,489, AND 0204 IS THE SHORTEST CHAPTER IN THE ACCOUNT SINCE 0191 AND IS NOT PADDED.** The three day-counts at 10 November, added up from the anchors and not carried forward: **349 / 263 / 265**, against 301 / 215 / 217 at the close.

**0195 — A WEEK AFTER THE DRAWING WENT OUT, AND WHAT A PLACE LOOKS LIKE WHEN A DRAWING OF IT IS ON THE WALL NEXT DOOR.** Nothing has happened to anybody on Peverell Street since Friday and there are more people on it than there were, and nobody can say why. A place that has been discussed is a place that has been dismissed. **THE FINDING IS THAT SOMEBODY USED THE DRAWING TO DO A CORRECT THING BEFORE ANYBODY ASKED AND IT CANNOT BE CHECKED BY ANYTHING** — a doorway at the bottom of a yard is dry, sandbagged two deep, with a new ferrule on a stopcock and a key in the hole, and no way whatever to show that it happened because the sheet has no address on it and not one word about a street, and the going the other way is the same, because a loss in a yard would also be untraceable. He puts a corner of a sheet back against a shutter.

**0196 — A MUTUAL-AID PERMISSION ARRIVES FROM ANOTHER CITY AND IT IS ABOUT SOMEBODY.** Two pages, reference AM/15/221, correct in every sentence, signed at both ends with nothing missing under either signature, a schedule of nine names, a duty clerk of about fifty-two who is not the person who agreed to it and says so. **IT IS THE FIRST DOCUMENT IN NINE MONTHS WITH A PERSON'S NAME ON IT, AND THE GOOD NEWS IS THAT A PERSON THIS BOROUGH COULD NOT FIND HAS BEEN FOUND, AND NOBODY HERE ASKED FOR IT, AND IT IS NOT A RESCUE.** A man of about sixty-two walked out of a room in Kilburn Street in April and has been at Alderwick since August on fourteen-day contracts renewed six times, and about ninety pounds of his is in a tin in a drawer, and a duty clerk was sent a schedule to fill in and nobody had a reason to take one name out.

**0197 — THE THING THAT LEAVES.** A man of about thirty-seven, on nothing, told at ten past six in the morning at a yard entrance that he had fourteen days, having said yes on the Saturday, with the going rate in an envelope in the cab before he starts and more of it than the going rate here. **HE WENT, HE IS ALL RIGHT, HE IS PAID, AND HE IS GONE, AND THIS BOROUGH HAS NEVER HAD A MECHANISM FOR A PERSON COMING BACK AND THERE IS NOT GOING TO BE ONE.** He was told he would be back on the twentieth and came back on the twentieth and goes out on Thursdays and has not been asked one question. A man a price makes findable is a different man from a man a shape in a room makes findable, and the two are not in one sentence, and no thread and no debt and no rescue.

**0198 — A RAIN THAT IS NOT A SPILL, AND THE THING THAT IS NOT CORRECT AND IS NOT WRONG.** Rain from the west, heavy until about half past seven on Monday 12 October, nobody died, nobody went to hospital, nobody was sent for, the water gone by the Wednesday. The correct things got done by the same four or five people, because there is nothing in this city that could put a sixth person into a yard at ten to five in the morning. **AT ABOUT TEN TO FIVE SOMEBODY PULLED THREE BOARDS OFF THE BOTTOM OF AN OLD BRICK BASE AND THE WATER WENT THROUGH AND THE WALL STOOD, AND HE HEARD IT AND TURNED ROUND AND SAW A LIGHT AND A SET OF BOOTS AND DID NOT SEE A FACE AND DID NOT ASK.** Nobody found out, and that is the correct outcome, and the boards went under the run and were not seen again.

**0199 — TOMAS RENN IS IN THE ROOM AND THE QUESTION IS NOT HIS TO ANSWER.** Three asks minuted and still correct, and **the third of them is clause one, clause three and the field in clause two with four things in it, and he asked for it in March and is not withdrawing it.** A permission and an obligation are opposites; one is a door and the other is a hand on it; this borough has been standing on the hand for nine months and the hand does not need a reason. **Jonas Mercer is right too and cannot improve it and does not try**, and then says the one true thing he has: nobody has ever said no to any of the three, and that is why he has not got them. **Renn says his own question is going to be put to somebody in the spring, and it is not going to be Jonas Mercer, and Jonas Mercer is not going to be in the room, and he says that in front of nine people on purpose.** Yolanda Fofana is not named and is not in a room.

**0200 — NELL ARDENT IS ASKED FOR SOMETHING BY A PERSON WHO IS NOT JONAS MERCER, AND IT IS A PERMISSION AND NOT A DRAWER.** A landlord of about seventy-six with a question that has a person's name in it and ninety pounds he cannot hand over. **SHE GIVES A CORRECT ANSWER THAT NOBODY CAN ACT ON AND SHE IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THE ROOM WHO IS AND BEING RIGHT IS NOT A FINDING AND IS NOT A VICTORY AND IS NOT A LOSS.** The four boxes with PARTY on them stay empty, nobody may ask her about the drawer, and her arc pays for nothing. He was in the corridor and not in the room.

**0201 — THE BOROUGH ANSWERS IT CORRECTLY AND DOES NOT WRITE IT DOWN, AND THIS TIME IT IS ON PURPOSE.** A canteen hatch opened on a Friday afternoon for six years on a woman's own unpaid time; and this time she wrote five words on the back of a bread delivery docket and put it in a drawer that has no lock on it and never has. **THE CARD FOR THIS CHAPTER CARRIES NO COUNT AND NEITHER DOES THE CHAPTER: A DRAFT CALLED IT *THE ELEVENTH TIME* AND GAVE JONAS MERCER A RUNNING TALLY SINCE THE SPRING, AND *THE ELEVENTH TIME* IS A CLOSED FIGURE AND A TALLY IS THE ONE INSTRUMENT THIS BOROUGH HAS SPENT NINE MONTHS REFUSING TO BUILD, AND BOTH WERE CUT.** **THAT IS THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT A CORRECT THING IN THIS BOROUGH HAS COME WITH A PIECE OF PAPER ON IT AND THE PERSON WHO PAID FOR THE PAPER WAS THE PERSON WHO DID THE THING, and he saw her do it and she knew he saw her do it and neither of them said a word, and no list of people who cannot be found has been built in this borough in nine months and one has not been started.** A level of service, once, in the head, about the printed row of 19 June, in a head that now opens on the canteen hatch and reaches the printed sheet in its second clause, so the one permitted use survives and the chapter is no longer liftable out of a state file.

**0202 — THE PERSON WHO WAS FOUND DID NOT ASK TO BE FOUND, AND HE SAYS THE SENTENCE HIMSELF.** He came across the estuary on Monday morning on his own account to find out why his name was on a piece of paper in a city he had left, and on Tuesday he said it twice and it was true both times and **NOBODY OBJECTED.** A woman of about thirty-eight got as far as the true thing — a permission finds people the same way the August document finds them, only with better handwriting — and stopped, because that is heard as an objection. **And he said the second half, which is the part nobody wanted: a doctor in a town he cannot name, paid for by a council that has never heard of him, correct, and he cannot say no to it, and he would like to, and the only way out is to write and he has not written anything to anybody since he was twenty-two.** Nobody rescued. Nobody sent for. Being counted in two cities did not help him and made the sentence heavier.

**0203 — THE DRAWING IS ON MORE WALLS THAN IT WAS AND THE SQUARE IS STILL EMPTY, AND SOMEBODY ASKS HIM ABOUT IT IN FRONT OF OTHER PEOPLE.** A woman with the key to a hall asked him which square is the empty one and what is supposed to go in it, and he said he does not know, and that he read the sheet on 18 September before it went on any wall, and that it has been empty on every copy since, and that he is not going to guess. **THAT IS THE FIRST TIME IN SEVEN WEEKS HE HAS SAID ANYTHING ABOUT IT TO ANYBODY.** The square is not filled, he was given nothing, the word *warden* is nowhere in the batch, and a room can hold a sentence and a room cannot hold a person.

**0204 — AN ORDINARY EVENING, A SMALL CORRECT THING MADE OF TWO PEOPLE, AND THE BATCH'S OWN QUESTION RESTATED AS A FACT THAT IT IS STILL OPEN.** A man of about fifty-four and a woman of about eighty-five clear a gully outside a door for about an hour and say three sentences to each other and neither of them is on anything and nobody is going to find out. The permission is in force, the drawing is on more walls than it was and nobody can say how many, the river is down, and everything that happened in seven weeks was correct and none of it is written down. **A permission is a thing a person may do. An obligation is a thing a person has to do. Nobody in this borough has worked out what either of them was handed. It is the tenth of November and it is open.**

**AND THE FIGURES, THE MOTIFS AND THE CAPS, ALL MEASURED FROM THE CHAPTERS AFTER THE REPAIRS: FIVE LATTICE NOTICES IN TEN CHAPTERS, ONE EACH IN 0195, 0197, 0198, 0201 AND 0204, AGAINST A CAP OF SIX, DEFINING NOTHING, REQUIRING NOTHING AND DEFINING *HOLDER* ON NONE. NO `---` SCENE BREAKS IN ANY OF THE TEN. TWO, FOUR, THREE, THREE, THREE, TWO, TWO, TWO, THREE AND TWO STANDALONE ALL-CAPS PARAGRAPHS PLUS THE NOTICE, AGAINST A CAP OF FIVE PER CHAPTER, AND EVERY CHAPTER IS AT OR UNDER IT. *THERE IS NO FORM IN THIS BOROUGH* 0 IN ALL TEN AND THAT IS THE MOST DELIBERATE MEASUREMENT IN THE BATCH BECAUSE THE ORDER IS THE SINGLE BIGGEST TEMPTATION IN THE WRITING OF IT AND IT IS DESCRIBED IN THREE CHAPTERS WITHOUT IT. *FOUR HUNDRED YARDS* 0. *ELEVEN FEET BY NINE* 0 AND DELIBERATELY, BECAUSE THE COUNTER IS IN NO SCENE. *NOBODY IS REQUIRED* 0 IN EIGHT CHAPTERS AND 2 IN 0196 AND 2 IN 0204, AND TWO CHAPTERS ARE AT THE CAP. *A LEVEL OF SERVICE* ONCE, IN A HEAD, ABOUT THE PRINTED ROW. THE ONLY *ELEVENS* ARE CLOCK TIMES. *IN ABOUT FOUR YEARS* AND *FOUR YEARS* AT ZERO. NO 48-MINUTE FIGURE. NO CHAPTER USES FORTY FOR A DURATION. NO SELF-REFERENCE TO A CHAPTER, A VOLUME, A BATCH, AN ACCOUNT, A CLOSE OR A READER SURVIVES IN ANY OF THE TEN. THE PERMITTED SAINT ORRA GATE SENTENCE WAS NOT USED AT ALL AND NO TERM AFTER THE TWENTY-THIRD WAS NAMED OR DATED. THE WORD *WARDEN* IS NOWHERE. NOBODY REFUSES TO BE THANKED AND THE ONLY THANKS IN THE BATCH ARE A REAL ONE ON A STAIRCASE AND A REAL ONE INSIDE A DOCUMENT.**

**TWENTY NEW FIGURES, EVERY AGE UNOCCUPIED IN ITS GENDER, AND NO TWO OF THE SAME GENDER SHARING AN AGE IN THE BATCH: MEN 28, 31, 37, 54, 57, 62, 67, 71, 76, 77, 79 AND WOMEN 24, 27, 32, 36, 38, 42, 52, 64, 85, PLUS TWO ONE-LINE SPEAKERS WITH NO AGE. SIX AGES THAT HAD BEEN DRAFTED AT OCCUPIED FIGURES WERE MEASURED OUT AND RE-AGED IN THE PROSE, INCLUDING ONE INSIDE A PRINTED DOCUMENT AND IN THREE MORE CHAPTERS — ASHGROVE THIRTY-SIX TO SIXTY-TWO, THE LANDLORD FORTY-THREE TO SEVENTY-SIX, THE CLINIC VOLUNTEER SIXTY-FOUR TO SEVENTY-ONE, THE MAN IN THE FLOODED YARD THIRTY-FOUR TO SEVENTY-SEVEN, THE CANTEEN WORKER FIFTY-EIGHT TO SIXTY-FOUR, AND A FIFTH — WHICH IS THE SEVENTH TIME IN THIS ACCOUNT THAT A STATE FILE OR A DRAFT WOULD HAVE CARRIED AN OCCUPIED FIGURE FORWARD, AND THE ONLY TIME THE FIGURE CENSUS WAS BUILT FROM EVERY AGE IN EVERY STATE FILE AND EVERY CHAPTER OF VOLUMES 01 TO 04 AND RUN AGAIN AFTERWARDS, AND IT CAME BACK WITH NO COLLISION IN EITHER GENDER.**

## Volume 05, Batch 0002 — Chapters 205–214, Wednesday 11 November to Wednesday 30 December 2015

**THE BATCH'S SHAPE, IN ORDER: THE DAY A DATE ON A PIECE OF PAPER ARRIVED AND NOTHING HAPPENED, AND WHAT THAT COST A PERSON WHO WAS NOT TOLD; AND THEN THE FIRST TIME ANYBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT WENT ANYWHERE WITH ANYBODY.**

**0205 — THE ELEVENTH OF NOVEMBER, AND THE THING NOBODY DID.** All seven clauses of the order made in this city on 13 August 2015 stopped being in force at about nine in the morning, all at once, and nobody did anything, nobody was asked, and nothing was cancelled. The mechanism is a person: a clause that says an order is renewed, or is not renewed, needs a person, and there is no body in this city that can require a person, and there are two people here with a department and neither is going to be asked to sign anything. **The field was not emptied and nobody wiped it; it is a column with things in it in a working book on a table, and the last entry in it is the fourteenth of September, which is the last time anybody in this city was called on.** **The thing nobody says out loud: for fifty-four days the only reason Jonas Mercer had anything to do about clause six was that the order was in force to be stopped, and on the morning of the eleventh it stopped being in force to be stopped, and a man of about twenty-three with a bad back is not a man who has been let go, and his back is in nobody's field because there is no field, and nobody in this city was told that this is what happened and nobody could be told.** Three people know the mechanism and none of them is going to be asked again. The line under the nineteenth is still blank, the register is still at nineteen, and that is the first finding of the eleventh of November and it is not a relief.

**0206 — WHAT A LAPSED INSTRUMENT LEAVES, FOUND OUT THE ONLY WAY THIS BOROUGH FINDS ANYTHING OUT.** Fifty minutes on foot and nothing had changed. A man of about seventy-three, called nine times since 22 August, paid the going rate, has declined nothing, was not called again, and looks different in a way Jonas Mercer could not put in a sentence: **he had his coat over his arm and nothing in his hands.** A man of about twenty-eight came back to a counter he had been at in June and asked whether there is anywhere a person can put the low end of Peverell Street, and the answer he got, twice and in the same words, was *there's no form for that*, and he said *I know*, and that was the whole of a four-minute meeting. **The counter answered nothing, gave no count and was not asked about the order, a gate, a rota, a person, a permission or a lapse.** He said one other thing, once, to the floor — that since the middle of September there has been no reason on any street in this city for anybody to be asked anything at all — and told nobody, including Jonas Mercer, who stood at the other end of the vestibule and said one word, which was none.

**0207 — SOMEBODY ASKS HIM TO GO SOMEWHERE, AND HE SAYS HE WILL GO BUT NOT ALONE, AND THAT IS NOT THE SAME AS SAYING YES TO A PARTY.** A one-page letter, signed by Iselin Krogh, about fifty-one, clerk of a standing group of three authorities that runs the water for three towns in another city, whose fourth paragraph reads *We record you as our correspondent on the above*. There is no budget, nothing to book a seat on, and nothing in this borough that can make him be in a room in another city. **He says out loud, before anybody asks him anything, to three people in one day, that he is going as a person who is there and not as anybody's delegate, and the arrangement has no word for that; and Tomas Renn says it is the first time anybody has said that sentence to him before being asked, in nine months, and that he has been on the other side of it about nine times and has never once been told in advance.** Sanaa Iqbal's boundary, the man-of-about-twenty-three's bad back, the empty square and the permission are all untouched. **And nobody has worked out how that group heard of a two-page sheet that went down on a counter by the water and went back across the estuary in a plastic wallet, and the permission from Alderwick is the reason the letter exists, and that is open.**

**0208 — FOUR PEOPLE ON A BOAT, AND ONE OF THEM SETTING A BOUNDARY OUT LOUD BEFORE ANYBODY HAS SAID ANYTHING ELSE.** Jonas Mercer, Tomas Renn, Nell Ardent and Sanaa Iqbal, on the early boat at ten past seven, none of them in a room with the other three since September. **The boundary is said in a doorway at half past six in the morning, in advance, by the woman who is not going to be fixed and is not going to be the reason anybody is talked out of anything, and she gives the reason she is on the boat and there is not a second reason underneath it; it is not a quarrel and none of the four improves it.** Nobody read anything out; there was no list, no roll and no names in order. Ardent is sick over the side. **And Jonas Mercer tells Tomas Renn, on the boat, because Renn will find out in about six weeks, that the order is not in force, and that the reason he had anything to do about clause six for fifty-four days was that it was in force to be stopped — and Renn refuses to be the one who says the rest of it out loud, because then it would be in Jonas's mouth for the rest of his life and he would be a man who was relieved of something.** They land in Ravensmere at ten past eight and the volume's major movement has begun.

**0209 — THE FIRST REGIONAL SYSTEM, AND THE FIRST THING IN IT THAT IS ABOUT OWNERSHIP.** It works, it is cheap, it is correct, it has not failed, and every part of it is held by somebody privately: a company in the first town, a trust in the second, and in the third one pumping station municipal and the other two not, and the two that are not have both changed hands inside ten years with nothing broken and nothing promised. **Nobody in it is a villain, nobody is hiding anything, and every sentence Iselin Krogh says is right — including the word *deliberate*, out loud, in a room where nobody disagreed.** The sheet says, at the bottom, in the same type, the same size, with the same space above each: *Nothing is required of any person in order to use any part of this system* — and *A person has no standing in respect of anything recorded under this.* **A person is not required to give anything up to use it, and a person cannot do anything about what happens to what they have given, and both of those are in the same two lines, and a man of about thirty-one who comes to a canteen hatch at ten to three on Fridays in this city, and is on nothing, puts them next to each other in a corridor and then wishes he had not:** *That's two lines. — That's two lines. — And they've been two lines since before the filters. — I'd rather I hadn't said that. Not because it's wrong. Because I've just said the only thing anybody's said about that wall in about ten years and I'm in a corridor.* He went back in and said nothing else all morning. He is not a thread.

**0210 — THE SECOND PART OF THE SAME SYSTEM, AND A PERSON WHO IS BEING PAID BY TWO PLACES.** A man of about thirty-nine is the last name on the schedule of the permission, works four days a week at a pumping station in this city, is paid by another city in an envelope at that city's rate, and crosses the estuary to be paid. **The water undertaker here calls it a relief contract and the other city's sheet calls it an attachment and defines an attached man as a man attached to the works, and he is the only person in the world who knows the difference, and nobody in this city could produce either piece of writing even if they were asked.** He is owed nothing, he is on nothing, and he is not a priced-access case and not a recognition case. **And Jonas Mercer stood in the corridor, could have asked him one question, and did not, and is not going to, and the envelope was still sealed when the man went down in the lift.** The group's own item that afternoon noted the number the arrangement had moved — six — and read out the sentence about where a claim arising out of the work is made, and nobody argued with it.

**0211 — SOMEBODY IS RIGHT ABOUT SOMETHING THAT CANNOT BE SAID IN THE ROOM SHE IS IN.** After fifty minutes of ordinary argument about standpipe hours, settled on seven until eight, which is nobody's number, **Iselin Krogh says the true thing about the bargain into a corridor, to one person, cannot finish the sentence, says it anyway, and then says she should not have said it, and it is not taken back: the part everybody is responsible for will always be the part somebody owns, the obligations were written first, the ownership came afterwards in pieces over a generation in rooms she was not in, nobody has done anything wrong, every part fits, the water is good — and the result of it, which nobody has ever put in a sentence in that room, is that the whole of the part of this that three towns are answerable for is the part that nobody owns, and the part nobody owns is the part that gets sold.** She could not finish it because in that room the sentence is an accusation and it is not an accusation, and she said it because the man she said it to has no standing, no office, no department and no body behind him. **He said nothing at all, the notebook stayed in the inside pocket of his coat, and on the Friday he told Nell Ardent *nothing's wrong with it, the water's good*, and it stayed in the corridor where she put it.** Nobody was given authority and nobody's sentence improved. **The standing of that sentence in this account is exactly the standing of a sentence said once on a pavement in September.**

**0212 — AN ORDINARY DAY, A SMALL CORRECT THING DONE BY TWO PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT ON ANYTHING, AND IT IS THE THIRD TIME.** A queue outside a chemist's, where a man of about eighty-four is wrong about the bottle and right about the label and says *a label's a card with a price on it*, and a bus shelter four hundred yards up the same street, where a woman of about sixty-nine is right about a timetable of the wrong year and will not shout at a driver about a printed sheet on a Sunday. **Neither is on anything, nobody found out, and they were four hundred yards apart and neither knew anything about the other, and the only person who knows both things happened walked down the same street twice.** This is the third time since the pump at the bottom of Peverell Street that a correct thing has been done in a street by somebody on nothing and nothing has come of it, and 0212 names the first two in its own head so the count is true on the page.

**0213 — THE THING THAT COMES BACK, AND IT IS NOT A PERSON.** A claim, one sheet, dated 11 December, carrying the reference of the permission, made in Alderwick under the sixth clause and correctly made, naming a man of about twenty who worked fourteen days at a pumping station on the north bank in October and was injured on the last day, for a sum that is about what a man of that age costs to keep for a month. **It has come to the city that granted the permission, which is the city the person came from, and it cannot be dealt with here, and it is in a tray under a window in a records room.** **Nobody in this borough can tell whether it is a price, a receipt or a precedent, and all three readings are true and are not the same shape.** Jonas Mercer is the first person to see what it is and does not say so and does not go and find the man. **By the time somebody else sees what it is there are two of them in two cities — he telephones Ravensmere and Iselin Krogh says she has had a letter with the same reference since the eighteenth and will not say what it is on a telephone — and there is no document in either of those two cities that says that either of them knew.** The man of about thirty-seven is not the man this is about and is not in one sentence with him.

**0214 — AN EVENING, AND THE BATCH'S QUESTION RESTATED AS A FACT ABOUT TWO DOCUMENTS, AND IT IS STILL OPEN.** Two pieces of paper in two hands in a room over a shop in Ravensmere: the office sheet in a plastic wallet and two sheets in Nell Ardent's own writing, copied off the corridor wall sheet and off the office sheet and compared with a ruler. **The wall sheet carries one line. The office sheet carries two. The short one has had a line taken off it and has not been left blank, and the four inches underneath are white and they are not white because there was nothing there.** And that is the batch's question as a fact: a permission is a thing a person may do, an obligation is a thing a person has to do, every one of the words on both sheets is a permission, and on the same sheet in the same type it says that whatever the water made a note of is not yours to have a say about, and that is one system with a front door and no back, and nobody in either city can say which of the two the people were handed. **Sanaa Iqbal says nobody is required to ask, and that everybody who comes to that standpipe is better off than they would be if somebody stood at the end of it with a clipboard, and that in about six months one of them is going to be tempted to make this into a fight about the sheet, and it is not a fight about the sheet, it is a fight about whether two sentences that are both true can be printed one above the other — and the answer to that is yes, and I do not like it, and there is nothing anybody can do about it before the end of the week.** And nothing was renewed, nobody named anything, nothing was minuted, the order is not in force, the permission is, the regional system is not going to explain itself before the end of the week, nobody was rescued and nobody died.

## Volume 05, Batch 0003 — Chapters 215–224

**Ten chapters, Wednesday 6 January to Sunday 21 February 2016, six weeks. 1,878 / 2,322 / 1,611 / 2,115 / 1,968 / 1,350 / 1,904 / 1,568 / 1,591 / 1,627 words, total 17,934, median 1,753 being the mean of the middle two, 1,627 and 1,878, by `wc -w` on the ten files whole after every repair and after the twenty-one review repairs and the eleven second-review repairs listed at the end of this summary. The gaps are 6, 5, 5, 6, 5, 5, 5, 5 and 4 days, all ten weekdays checked against the real 2016 calendar, and no chapter is dated before Wednesday 6 January 2016.**

**THE BATCH'S SHAPE, IN ORDER: THE SECOND OF THE THREE REGIONAL SYSTEMS AND THE FIRST THING IN IT THAT IS A PERSON; THAT MAN AND WHAT IT COSTS AND WHO PAYS IT; AN ORDINARY DAY IN THAT TOWN WHICH IS NOT A CRISIS; THE BILL FOR A YEAR; THE DELEGATION; THE BOUNDARY; THE CLAIM FROM A SECOND DIRECTION; A ROOM IN THIS BOROUGH; AN EVENING AND A FACT ABOUT A PERSON; AND A SUNDAY THAT ENDS ON A FACT AND NOT ON A RESOLUTION.**

**THE SECOND SYSTEM IS THE EXACT MIRROR OF THE FIRST AND EVERY STEP OF ITS ARGUMENT IS CORRECT. IN RAVENSMERE THE OWNERSHIP IS PRIVATE AND THE OBLIGATION IS PUBLIC AND THERE IS NOBODY TO HOLD IT. IN MARROWGATE, A TOWN OF ABOUT THREE THOUSAND EIGHT MILES UP THE ESTUARY, THE WORKS, THE CLINIC, THE SHELTER, THE GATE AND THE YARD ALL BELONG TO THE TOWN, A HOUSE HAS A WATER BILL OF ABOUT NINETY PENCE A QUARTER, NOBODY WHO LIVES THERE HAS EVER BEEN ASKED FOR A CARD, AND THE WHOLE BILL IS A SEVENTH OF WHAT THE SAME WATER COSTS ON THIS SIDE OF THE ESTUARY. A DUTY IS AN ACT AND NOT A PLACE, AND A PLACE CANNOT BE GIVEN TO A PERSON, AND THEREFORE THE PERSON CANNOT BE REPLACED, AND THEREFORE NOBODY HAS EVER ASKED HIM. THE LAST STEP IS NEVER DRAWN OUT LOUD AND IT IS TRUE. A MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-SIX IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THAT TOWN WHO COULD READ A PUMP HOUSE, STRIP A STOPCOCK, RELIGHT THE SHELTER BOILER, WORK THE INTAKE RACK AND CHANGE THE CHAIN ON THE YARD GATE, AND HE IS NOT THE ONLY PERSON WHO DOES ANY OF IT, AND HE FOUND THAT OUT ON THE WEDNESDAY AFTERNOON OF 12 JANUARY AND TOLD NOBODY. HE WAS NOT RESCUED, HE WAS NOT ASKED TO STOP, AND NOTHING WAS ARRANGED FOR HIM. THE ONE PHYSICAL THING THAT COSTS SOMEBODY A DAY'S WORK AND THAT NO DOCUMENT IN THAT TOWN RECORDS IS THAT THERE IS NO TANK BIG ENOUGH, SO THE WHOLE TOWN IS DRY WHILE THE INTAKE IS RAKED, AND A WOMAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY WHO TAKES IN WASHING HAS STOPPED WASHING ON THOSE TWO DAYS WITHOUT TELLING ANYBODY. THE BILL FOR THE NINE DAYS HE WAS OFF IN FEBRUARY OF THE YEAR BEFORE WAS THREE DAYS OF A MAN, A FORTNIGHT OF HIS WAGES AND A TANK, AND THE FIGURE FOR THE WORKS HAS NOT MOVED IN ELEVEN JANUARIES BECAUSE THERE IS NO NINE DAYS LINE, AND THE CORRECT SENTENCE ABOUT THAT SOUNDS LIKE AN OBJECTION, AND FOUR PEOPLE IN THAT ROOM THOUGHT IT AT THE SAME MOMENT, AND NOBODY PUT IT IN THE ROOM, AND JONAS MERCER WAS IN THE CORRIDOR OUTSIDE IT ALL DAY AND IS THE ONLY PERSON IN TWO COUNTRIES WHO COULD HAVE.**

**WHAT THE BATCH SPENT: a white van across two gates a month apart, five hours of a Sunday lost on foot and six boxes carried in on a sheet, and two people who were never told about each other; a sentence about a firm that got one room further and a question that was not answered, and a bar that came off a back door and cannot go back on; two jobs on one landing that each wanted a second pair of hands, and a man in a ground floor flat who came out twice in his socks; two wrong versions of a collection day and about sixty notices nobody has read; a fifth thing a building did to a woman who is not counted, a bowl she put under it herself, and a wheelie bin hooked back onto two studs four hundred yard off; three gates in clear wrappers left in three yards on one lane in two months, a number of three said out loud for the first time in anybody's life, and a barrow going up that lane at six in the morning with the last of them on it; three lengths of close-board against a brick wall, a promise made at a door that the man who made it does not deny making, and a quarter of an inch written on the edge of a sheet and rubbed out; a carpet laid for three weeks and stood up against a wall, a chest put down on a step and refused in about a second by a person who was not asked why; one day in five rooms with four of the five full of people, in which a lad of about eighteen was asked nothing and said one thing out loud in front of three of them and was answered by nothing; and an ordinary Saturday in July, with a register at nineteen and nine hundred and fifty-five days under the last entry and a blank line, four answers still going round, a question from a man who had never been in that room, and nobody answering anything.**

A handrail goes back onto a landing wall in the dark on a Friday night, on a landing that has never had a light on it, and the one person who could give it away is told it is sound and answers about a kettle. Two men count four hundred and seventy-odd slabs in a yard, both counts honest, sixteen of them the whole of the difference, and two numbers end up on a slate in two hands. A man leaves one tile on a stone shelf where a man he knows walks past twice a day and watches him not see it twice. A door left open behind a hall is shut and chained by the last man out and a man who was at his sister's writes a sentence about it with the wrong hour in it, which nobody can ever fix. And a fortnight passes and the only thing that moves is a chair, and a man stands at the end of a landing and works out that a line in a book has been empty for one thousand and seventy days.**

CARD 0363 ASKS FOR A RECORD WRITTEN BY SOMEBODY WHO WAS NOT IN THE ROOM, AND THE ENGINE IS AN HOUR, WHICH IS AN ORDINARY DETAIL AND NOT A FINDING.**

**THE FIVE FLAGS GO TO A HUMAN AND NONE OF THEM IS A WRITER'S. THE NAME OF THE CITY. WHAT VOLUME 05 IS CENTRALLY. THE BOUNDARY, WHICH IS A HUMAN'S IN ONE LINE AND WHICH CHAPTERS MAY NOT DECIDE, MOVE OR HIDE.
